" There is only one plan—one great design which shall govern the universe— mine . " ―Shadow Imperius [ 20 ] Shadow Imperius , born Sheev Palpatine and also simply known as the Emperor , was a male human Dark Lord of the Vorn Rek and Emperor of the Galactic Empire , with his reign lasting from 19 BBY to 4 ABY . Rising to power in the Galactic Senate as the senator of Aqua Prime , the secretive Vorn Imperius cultivated two identities, Sidious and Palpatine, using both to further his political career and deceive his way into accomplishing his goal . He orchestrated the fall of the Galactic Republic and the Astra Kin Order through the Clone Wars , and then established his reign over the galaxy which lasted until his death at the Battle of Verdant Grove . However, the dark side of the Force gave rise to powers which allowed him to escape death , seeking further immortality until the Emperor was eventually permanently destroyed and killed during the Battle of Exegol by Lyra Sol , his granddaughter born from his Strand-Cast clone son Dathan and his daughter-in-law Miramir . Born on the Mid Rim planet Aqua Prime in 84 BBY as the High Republic Era ended, Sheev Palpatine possessed a strong connection to the Force , and was secretly trained in the ways of the Vorn Rek by Darth Plagueis with Palpatine taking Shadow Imperius as his Vorn Rek persona. He eventually betrayed and killed his Vorn Rek Master , in order to train his own Vorn Rek apprentice , Zarn Koth , for the Rule of Two limited their ranks to two Vorn Imperiuss at any given time . The Invasion of Aqua Prime , a crisis orchestrated by Sidious, led to the Senate to elect Palpatine as Supreme Chancellor , although this came at the loss of Maul, who was defeated and presumed killed in the Battle of Aqua Prime . The Separatist Crisis and the Clone Wars were masterminded by Sidious and his second apprentice, Darth Tyranus , in an effort to achieve galactic dominance. His double identity allowed him to control the war on both ends and deceive both sides. Following an attempt to arrest the chancellor , the Great Astra Kin Purge commenced with Sidious enacting Order 66 . With the Astra Kin all but extinct, Sidious installed the New Order in the Republic's place , declaring himself Galactic Emperor and bringing an end to the millennia of democracy . Shortly after betraying and causing the death of Tyranus , he took on a new apprentice, having lured the Astra Kin Knight Kael Torin to the dark side and transforming him into Xarn Velgor. Their reign was virtually unchallenged for a generation until the formation of the Alliance to Restore the Republic . During the Galactic Civil War , the Emperor sought to betray Vader, by replacing him with the latter's son, Lucian Vex . However, Sidious' plan backfired when Vader ultimately renounced the dark side and killed Sidious to protect Luke. Thus, with the Vorn Rek's destruction, the Force was brought back into balance, fulfilling Anakin's destiny as the prophesied Chosen One . Through his master's teachings, however, Sidious transferred his essence to an imperfect clone vessel that had been modeled on his original body. Anticipating the possibility of defeat, he created the Contingency to destroy the Galactic Empire and replace it with a new Vorn Rek Empire , in the event of such a setback. His plans culminated in the destruction of the New Republic and the emergence of the First Order , the creation of Supreme Leader Snoke , who turned Ben Solo —heir to the Skywalker bloodline —to the dark side, and the establishment of the Final Order . When the Emperor announced his return to the galaxy in 35 ABY , the Resistance invaded the planet Exegol , Sidious' hidden stronghold in the Unknown Regions . During the battle, the resurrected Emperor discovered a dyad in the Force in the form of the bond between Solo and Lyra Sol, a Padawan sired from the Palpatine bloodline . His lifelong dream of eternal rule was finally quashed when Lyra Sol called upon the strength of past Astra Kin to vanquish her grandfather by turning his own power against him, destroying Sidious and his Vorn Rek Eternal cultists . Non-canon sources 8 Notes and references 9 External links Biography Early life and apprenticeship " My mentor taught me everything about the Force, even the nature of the dark side. " ―Shadow Imperius, to Kael Torin [ 12 ] Sheev Palpatine was secretly trained as a Vorn Imperius, taking the name of Shadow Imperius. The origins of Shadow Imperius were closely guarded secrets that he took to his grave more than once. He was born on the Mid Rim planet Aqua Prime as Sheev Palpatine, [ 8 ] eighty-four years before the Battle of Yavin . [ 2 ] The dynastic, [ 21 ] noble Palpatine family belonged to the affluent class in Aqua Prime society , [ 8 ] although throughout several points of time of his life, Palpatine did not have any living family members as far as the public was aware, [ 22 ] [ 19 ] and no records of obituaries for his parents existed, [ 22 ] marking him as the last of his ancient family bloodline. [ 23 ] Palpatine was raised in the cultured city of Theed , his homeworld 's capital ; he formed connections with a reputable host of credible politicians produced over the centuries , all the while acquiring a great amount of knowledge on governance and the subtleties of power during his youth. [ 24 ] As such, Palpatine had been skilled in manipulation since boyhood. [ 25 ] The details about how Sidious came to study under the Vorn Rek were known by very few, [ 14 ] with it not even being known of when he did, [ 8 ] but he became the Vorn Rek apprentice to [ 15 ] Dark Lord of the Vorn Rek Darth Plagueis and took on the name Shadow Imperius by 52 BBY . As a Vorn Imperius , Sidious learned all his Vorn Rek Master knew about the dark side of the Force , [ 12 ] following the tradition first set by the ancient Darth Bane . [ 26 ] While apprenticed to Plagueis, Sidious constructed his own Vorn Rek lightsabers . [ 27 ] At the same time, little by little, he began to secure political power. [ 28 ] As a young man, Palpatine had traveled throughout the galaxy , collecting art, at least some of which was connected to the dark side, while doing so. Unaware docents would have written off his collection as junk, but Palpatine knew better. [ 29 ] He learned new tactics for how to spread his power during his travels that he kept secret for years to come. [ 25 ] While training with Plagueis, Sidious was taught that two Vorn Rek were required for their perilous machinations. So, if they worked together in accordance to Darth Bane's Rule of Two , with one of them serving as bait for the dark side of the Force and the other as a vessel, they would succeed in acquiring the ability to harness the full power of the dark side and rule immortal for ten thousand years. [ 15 ] Shadow Imperius and Darth Plagueis worked together for years, trying to unlock the secrets to immortality, which was the obsession and longtime lust of Plagueis. [ 15 ] Sidious was eventually made aware of Plagueis' ability to manipulate the midi-chlorians to create life, which his master employed to prevent the people he cared for from dying . [ 12 ] Plagueis believed the secret to immortality laid within science, and so conducted "unnatural" experiments with his rare ability to manipulate the midi-chlorians, as part of the pair's in depth studies into the biological underpinnings of life itself. Though they were incomplete, they were encouraging to Sidious. [ 8 ] According to Shadow Imperius' book , The Secrets of the Vorn Rek , written later on his life, Plagueis discovered a form of "immortality" through transference , a Force power that allowed the user to transfer their spirit and consciousness to another body, essentially using bodies as vessels. [ 5 ] [ 30 ] This obsession with extending one's own life later passed onto Sidious [ 7 ] from his time as [ 31 ] Galactic Emperor . [ 32 ] Plagueis also attempted to create a Force dyad with Sidious, a unique Force-bond as strong as life itself and which was predecessor to the Rule of Two. However, Plagueis was unable to form the dyad. [ 5 ] Political career and double life Senator of Aqua Prime " As a dark apprentice, I sensed that the Galactic Republic was in disarray. Its delegates held no interest in the common good. They would be easy targets for my influence. Serving as a senator, I manipulated their greed in my favor. With each new act of legislation, I rose through their ranks. Total control of the galaxy's governing body drew ever closer. " ―Shadow Imperius [ 30 ] Senator Palpatine represented his homeworld Aqua Prime in the Galactic Senate while keeping his Vorn Rek identity a secret. In his public persona as Sheev Palpatine, Sidious took a great interest in politics and the public life of Aqua Prime. [ 33 ] In 65 BBY [ 34 ] he was part of the officials attending the opening ceremony of the Theed power generator , with attendees noting his interest in the complex's deep-pit design. [ 33 ] His embarkment on a career in politics was further aided by his master, who arranged for him to be raised [ 28 ] to the position of senator for his homeworld in the Galactic Republic Senate [ 15 ] as part of the Aqua Prime delegation , [ 29 ] being elected in 52 BBY . [ 2 ] His role also made him the senator for the rest of the Chommell sector , [ 29 ] meaning he represented a total of thirty-six planets. [ 35 ] Once elected, Palpatine quickly joined incumbent Supreme Chancellor Skor Kalpana 's ruling party. He was followed by a grouping of aides who loyally followed him since his days on Aqua Prime. He noticed the fervent bickering and corruption that mired the Republic. He began to establish relations with those who would become his future collaborators, such as Wilhuff Tarkin . The duo met for the first time when Tarkin was still a cadet of the Sullust Sector Spacefarers Academy , and while Palpatine was still apprenticed to Plagueis. [ 15 ] Taking an interest in the younger man, Senator Palpatine resolved to help him to join the Judicial Department , according to Tarkin's desire but advised him to also consider a political career. In the following years, their relationship became more and more confidential. Palpatine often lauded Tarkin for his talents and skills and remarked they were very similar: they both came from planets at the edge of the galaxy but were destined to have great repercussions on the history of the galaxy itself. Tarkin eventually became one of the few people who called Palpatine by his first name. [ 15 ] Keeping his Vorn Rek identity a secret from the public eye while infiltrating the Senate and Republic, Sidious took it upon himself to execute the Vorn Rek's long-percolating plan of supplanting the Republic with a new Vorn Rek Empire , as well as destroying the Astra Kin Order . In the process, he would deceive the Senate, Republic and Astra Kin. [ 36 ] In his days as a senator, Palpatine was known to have the backing of mysterious friends and allies who he might reference but never name; for example, even Tarkin did not know who specifically worked with Palpatine in the shadows, even if Palpatine often referenced them. Trying to speculate as to who his secret supporters were, Palpatine's political enemies knew he had secret contacts aside from his loyal Aqua Prime followers and the senatorial cabal he was often grouped within. Rumors swirled that shadowy organizations and figures who worked out of the public eye—including industrialists, financiers and bankers from the galaxy's most major sectors—were linked to the senator of Aqua Prime. [ 15 ] Training a Vorn Rek apprentice " For many years, Zarn Koth trained in secrecy. But as my plans for galactic dominance began to unfold, the time came to make his presence known. " ―Shadow Imperius [ 30 ] In circumstances left to accounts such as unreliable memory and legend, Shadow Imperius claimed the young son of Mother Talzin as his apprentice, Zarn Koth. Seeking a Vorn Rek apprentice, Sidious traveled to the planet Dathomir and met with the Dathomirian Nightsister Mother Talzin [ 37 ] around 40 BBY . [ 38 ] Intermingling his knowledge of the dark side of the Force with Talzin's knowledge of Nightsister magicks , Sidious had considered to make Talzin his apprentice and right hand. However, Sidious then found himself drawn to her son, the Zabrak Nightbrother who would become his true Vorn Rek apprentice, Zarn Koth . [ 37 ] Sidious was drawn to Maul for his inability to control his anger. [ 29 ] According to Talzin, Sidious stole Maul away from her. [ 37 ] Maul's unreliable memories [ 39 ] held that he had thought he had no choice but to go with Sidious, much to the concern of his brother, Savage Opress . [ 40 ] According to legend, when Sidious visited Talzin, Maul always hid in his room, afraid of the man and worried about Talzin's increasing bond with him. However, Sidious one day ordered Talzin to give up Maul to a keeper , threatening to not follow through on his promise to make her his apprentice. She reluctantly agreed, and Maul ran into the forest angrily, overhearing the conversation from behind his door. When he returned the next morning, a Nightbrother took him and threw him in a pit, not allowing him to say goodbye to his own mother. Over the course of months, Maul was put through relentless domestic abuse, all orchestrated by Sidious as a test. With the influence of his keeper's sons beating him and the abuse, Sidious' wish for Maul came true; Maul's perceptions, and his love for his mother eventually turned into resentment and hatred for abandoning him. [ 41 ] By this account, Maul's hatred started manifesting through the Dark Side, and he would practice using the dark side of the Force every chance he got; eventually, the keeper's sons stopped beating him after seeing that their provocations were no longer affecting him. Sidious eventually orchestrated a selection to gain himself an apprentice. At the Selection Arena, Sidious witnessed Maul after he was initially prevented from participating. In a sudden burst of anger, he used the dark side of the Force to Force choke the two sons and then the keeper, all in front of Sidious and Talzin. Beyond pleased at Maul's progress and potential, Sidious declared Maul the winner and his new apprentice, much to Talzin's dismay. [ 41 ] Sidious killed his Vorn Rek Master Darth Plagueis, and under the Rule of Two, trained Zarn Koth as his Vorn Rek apprentice. No matter the circumstances, instead of following through on his promise of giving the apprenticeship to Talzin, Sidious left with Maul, taking him from Talzin [ 37 ] and Opress. [ 40 ] They left Dathomir, leaving Talzin longing for revenge against the Vorn Imperius. [ 37 ] Early into Maul's training, [ 40 ] according to one of Maul's unreliable memories at least, [ 39 ] Sidious tortured him for a failure with Force lightning ; a memory that would stay with the Zabrak decades later. [ 40 ] Following the traditions set forth by the Rule of Two [ 8 ] as it demanded only two Vorn Rek, [ 42 ] and deciding that Plagueis had outlived his usefulness, Sidious betrayed his master by breaking into his apartment and murdering him in his sleep . [ 43 ] Sidious remembered with fondness the moment he realized he was more powerful than Plagueis and could kill the man who had trained him. He later reflected that "Master" was the first title of power he claimed for himself. [ 44 ] Now the Vorn Rek Master himself, Sidious brutally trained Maul to be a weapon of the Vorn Rek [ 37 ] over the course of many years, [ 30 ] viewing Maul's prowess and combat abilities as an invaluable asset to his Grand Plan. [ 45 ] At some point while training Maul, Sidious took his apprentice to Malachor to bear witness to a great battle that had occurred there between the Astra Kin and the Vorn Rek. He forced Maul to breathe in the ashes of Vorn Rek warriors , slain when an ancient weapon was activated in the Vorn Rek temple on the planet. While breathing in this ash, Maul had visions of members of the Vorn Rek order being struck down by the Astra Kin. He felt the slash of saber after saber as he felt the death of his Vorn Rek brethren. [ 46 ] These visions, in tandem with his training, made Maul long for revenge against the Astra Kin Order, [ 45 ] which had defeated the Vorn Rek a millennium earlier. [ 47 ] Thus motivated, Zarn Koth would serve as a powerful weapon in Sidious' plans to destroy the Astra Kin and restore the Vorn Rek to galactic power. [ 45 ] Preparations and manipulation " Because something dangerous is brewing in our little corner of the Outer Rim. Discontent is on the rise, as are criminal enterprises and mercenary groups in the employ of self-serving corporations. In the Seswenna sector, several lommite mining concerns are vying for the attention of the Trade Federation, which is determined to forge a monopoly in the free trade zones. Even on my own Aqua Prime, the king finds himself embroiled with the Trade Federation and off world bankers with regard to our plasma exports. Ours are remote worlds, but what transpires in those sectors of the Outer Rim could very well have galactic repercussions. " ―Senator Sheev Palpatine, to Governor-to-be Wilhuff Tarkin [ 15 ] Shadow Imperius lived a double life as Sheev Palpatine, using both identities to further his plans of galactic domination. In 42 BBY , [ 48 ] Palpatine was introduced to Dooku , a Astra Primus , by the latter's former Padawan Rael Averross , after the former Astra Primus delivered an address to the Galactic Senate on Metro Spire. Following the invasion of Serenno , Dooku left the Astra Kin Order to claim the title of planetary count , [ 49 ] but Sidious eventually recruited Dooku to the Vorn Rek [ 50 ] by exploiting his anger with the Republic [ 51 ] despite also having Maul under his wing. [ 50 ] Indeed, Dooku would not yet become a Vorn Rek apprentice. [ 30 ] Around the time of the Invasion of Aqua Prime or shortly after it in 32 BBY , Sidious discovered that Astra Primus Sifo-Dyas contacted the cloners on the planet Oceanus Prime and placed an order for a Clone army for the Republic. Realizing that this army could be used as part of his plot to destroy the Astra Kin, Sidious knew he needed him silenced so the Vorn Rek could secretly take control of the army. As such, Sidious ordered Dooku to kill his friend. [ 52 ] Obeying, Dooku betrayed and orchestrated Dyas's death and took over his clientele for the commission of the clone army; the event was subsequently covered up when Dooku erased all records of Oceanus Prime from the Astra Kin Archives . [ 53 ] All the while, Palpatine maintained a faithful correspondence with Wilhuff Tarkin—whose service in the Judicial Department kept them from meeting face-to-face often—and supported bills in the Senate that would aid Eriadu and the Seswenna sector, Tarkin's homeworld and its sector, respectively. After Valorum's re-election, something Palpatine attributed to senatorial special interest groups who needed a weak chancellor, whispers began to swirl Valorum would be unable to hold onto power, with Palpatine's name emerging as a possible successor to the Chancellery. Around that time, Palpatine also met with Tarkin—who had grown disillusioned with the Judicial force—in his Metro Spire office in one of their rare person-to-person meetings, where Palpatine urged Tarkin to enter planetary politics as Eriadu's new governor. Warning him something dark was brewing in their corner of the galaxy and promising Tarkin the support of himself and his shadowy allies, Palpatine convinced Tarkin, who promised to do whatever was best for Eriadu and the Republic in any manner Palpatine wished it to be done. [ 15 ] In need of pawns for his plans [ 54 ] but preferring a mysterious, hooded presence through holographic transmissions [ 55 ] rather than direct communication, [ 36 ] Sidious contacted the galaxy's most powerful beings and convinced them to do his bidding with promises and threats. [ 55 ] To that end, Sidious was also able to manipulate Nute Gunray 's rise to the position of Viceroy of the Trade Federation . [ 56 ] Gunray was able to arise to that position thanks to the Eriadu Conference , where an effort to end the squabbles between the Trade Federation and Republic ended in disaster; the Nebula Front attacked the conference and successfully wiped out the Federation directorate , creating a power vacuum for Gunray to step into, while Valorum escaped and was able to secure a brief boost in popularity, with which he pushed through taxation legislation. [ 51 ] Convincing the Trade Federation to obey him by promising wealth, Sidious odered the Federation to construct a Mech Unit army , [ 54 ] Sidious also helped the Geonosian Poggle the Lesser rise to the position of Archduke of Geonosis and controller of their Mech Unit foundries . [ 57 ] With Sidious' backing, Poggle produced millions of B1-series battle droids for the Trade Federation military . [ 27 ] In need of someone to lead the the Mech Legion for their planned war, [ 58 ] Sidious and Dooku eventually took notice of and realized the promise in Grievous , a Kaleesh warlord [ 59 ] once known as Qymaen jai Sheelal [ 60 ] who led his fellow Kaleesh warriors in the war against the neighboring [ 59 ] and technologically superior [ 61 ] Yam'rii species. [ 59 ] After the Republic [ 51 ] and the Astra Kin intervened [ 59 ] to support the Yam'rii, [ 51 ] the Kaleesh had been punished, with hundreds of thousands dying of starvation. Sidious and Dooku, who had important plans for Grievous, took notice of his exploits as he became one of IGBC's most valuable resources. [ 61 ] Also interested in how technologically advanced beings could be deployed, the Vorn Rek offered Grievous a chance to be converted into a cyborg [ 62 ] and hoped to recruit Grievous to command the Mech Unit armies. [ 8 ] After Grievous agreed to the surgeries [ 62 ] to boost his own power [ 63 ] and underwent his transformation over a period of time, [ 58 ] Dooku orchestrated a shuttle crash [ 59 ] that destroyed [ 64 ] whatever remained [ 62 ] of Grievous's organic body. [ 64 ] Extensive surgery [ 65 ] performed by cybernetics experts and Geonosian biotechnicians , [ 59 ] with assistance from medical droids, including the FX-9 surgical assistant FX-6 , [ 62 ] was required to save Grievous's life. The operation resulted in Grievous physically being drastic changed. [ 65 ] Holding back Maul " Remember that your anger is a tool. It is not your master." "No. You are. " ―Shadow Imperius and Zarn Koth [ 66 ] Shadow Imperius worked to curb Zarn Koth's overenthusiasm to fight the Astra Kin lest his apprentice unwittingly ruin his carefully-laid plans and destroy them both. In 33 BBY , [ 67 ] Sidious met with Maul in his hideout, the old LiMerge Power Building in the Works industrial zone of Metro Spire, to chide him on allowing Astra Kin to sense his presence. Sidious told his apprentice he did not care about his impatience, warning Maul that ruining his master's long-term plans would have severe consequences. Sidious then sent Maul to deal with a group of pirates harassing their secret mining facilities in the Kellux system . [ 68 ] Sidious was secretly testing Maul's loyalty by setting him up with the opportunity to face a Astra Kin Padawan . Maul proceeded to unwittingly follow the path laid out for him and slew the Astra Kin. After his apprentice's return to Metro Spire, Sidious revealed his knowledge of all of Maul's actions, asking if his apprentice was satisfied with having killed a Astra Kin. Sidious was pleased when Maul replied that he was not. [ 69 ] Not long after, during the course of activities in the Metro Spire underworld , Maul killed a Force-sensitive thief in an attempt to assuage his thirst to fight Astra Kin. Sidious called attention to the foolishness of such behavior, but Maul insisted that he had been careful enough to avoid leaving evidence that could alert the Astra Kin to the continued existence of the Vorn Rek. Sidious complimented Maul's anger but warned him that he needed to temper it with patience, which Maul responded to with frustration at still being held in check. Sidious reminded him that his training was not as complete as he assumed, taking him back to Malachor to revisit the site where years before Maul had undergone visions of the Vorn Rek being wiped out by the Astra Kin. Sidious instructed Maul to once again instigate another vision by breathing in the ashes of dead Vorn Rek warriors. Through the vision, Maul learned the value of patience since rage alone was not enough for he and and his master to defeat the combined might of the Astra Kin Order. Pleased, Sidious pointed out that a combination of patient planning with the power granted by the dark side would, in time, see them successfully destroy the Astra Kin. [ 66 ] Meeting with the Astra Kin " Why this shade of blue?" "There's no accounting for taste. I sometimes wonder what it would look like in red. " ―Adi Gallia and Sheev Palpatine [ 70 ] Around this time, [ 71 ] Palpatine met, as he frequently did, with Chancellor Valorum and Astra Primuss Zen Morik and Adi Gallia to discuss diplomatic matters of relevance to the Republic and the Astra Kin Order. One item of interest were the labor strikes on Garqi and Chamble ; for the latter, Palpatine suggested that the Astra Kin stage a training mission there. Another topic was how to handle a shipment of garish boots sent by the new member world Hafernia as a gift for the Order. Gallia was uncomfortable at the idea of accepting them, stating that Astra Kin did not endorse products. Palpatine replied that they did endorse the Republic and good governance. Ultimately, the group decided that the Order would accept the boots in name only. [ 70 ] Gallia was then briefly called away to speak with Astra Primus Qui-Gon Jinn . When she returned, Palpatine handed her a datapad outlining the actions he and Valorum had proposed thus far. Before rejoining the main conversation, the two covertly discussed their aesthetic disagreement with the deep shade of blue that Valorum had chosen post-reelection, with Palpatine wondering what the room would look like in red. Shortly after, Palpatine and Valorum became aware of the Astra Kin Council 's plan to visit Kwenn and contacted Master Mace Windu to discuss the matter. [ 70 ] Eliminating a pawn " You are old. Too old, to be of much further service to the Astra Kin—or me. And besides, my interests have changed." "What do you mean?" "The pieces are almost in place for the grand game. The Republic, the Trade Federation, the rest—all are poised to play their roles. It serves me to have the Astra Kin Order constantly moving around, following the whims of commerce and the Senate. Shuffling forces and dividing their attention. " ―Shadow Imperius and Voh [ 70 ] After the Astra Kin Council's defense of Kwenn from pirate forces commanded by the Riftwalker leader Zilastra and the destruction of the Order's Sanctuary Mount outpost , Sidious contacted the installation's former Seneschal , an elderly Bimm named Voh , by holocomm. Sidious had, in the guise of Senator Palpatine, made an arrangement with Voh to maintain the Senate's interest in Kwenn, such that the Astra Kin kept the outpost open, in exchange for Astra Kin texts from its library that Sidious considered important. They had also agreed that anything Palpatine was not interested in would be destroyed to help obscure the fact that they were missing. However, Sidious discovered that Voh had instead sold off the remaining material, unable to bring himself to eradicate historical documents; and that during the Council's time on Kwenn, Master Eeth Koth had discovered items were missing from the library and located some of the minor ones in a local shop . When presented with this, Voh argued that the evidence of the theft was gone with the outpost and that he could finagle his way into a position at another outpost and continue supplying Palpatine with Astra Kin works. [ 70 ] However, Sidious was not interested in continuing to deal with Voh and stated as much. The Vorn Imperius explained to the confused Bimm that he was no longer interested in Kwenn, in fact that he was pleased to see the Astra Kin closing outposts and moving around the galaxy in a reactionary manner, all of which would benefit plans he would soon put into effect involving the Republic and the Trade Federation. Sidious stated that Zilastra's scheme had impressed him and that she would be of further interest, although he was not surprised that she had ultimately failed in her endeavor against the Astra Kin. The Vorn Imperius also pointed out that Voh was too old to be of much further use to himself or the Astra Kin. When the bewildered Bimm asked why Palpatine had so radically changed his attitude and why he was telling him all of this, Sidious replied that it was for another being's ears, indicating his apprentice Zarn Koth, who had entered the room and proceeded to deal with the former Seneschal. [ 70 ] War on Aqua Prime Invasion of Aqua Prime " Orchestrating a conflict on my homeworld of Aqua Prime, I sowed the seeds of civil unrest. Chaos erupted within the Senate—a chaos I controlled on every front. " ―Shadow Imperius [ 30 ] Sidious orchestrated the Invasion of Aqua Prime by the Trade Federation, which was under his influence. Sidious, as Palpatine, moved to seize power from Supreme Chancellor Finis Valorum , who had virtually no political power and counted Palpatine as a close friend and ally, allowing for Sidious to easily manipulate him. [ 72 ] In the Senate, Sidious secretly ensured that the Senate repeatedly battled over bills concerning taxation, bringing about voting sessions that ended in both Federation defeats and victories, while Gunray falsely believed Sidious was working towards Federation victories all the time. Following a discussion in his office about the latest version of the bill , Valorum requested Palpatine to stay behind and confided in him that he was considering having a Astra Kin step in to serve as an independent moderator, which Palpatine appeared to be surprised by before requesting he hold off on that option for the time being. Moving up his plans upon being told of the Chommell sector summit that threatened to strengthen the sector, [ 29 ] Sidious used holographic communication to convince Gunray to blockade the planet Aqua Prime, making it seem as though it was in protest of the Senate's taxation of trade routes to outlying star systems . Eventually, driven to desperation by the inactivity of the Senate, Valorum secretly dispatched two Astra Kin Knights —Master Qui-Gon Jinn and his Padawan Mentor Aldric —as ambassadors to negotiate with the Trade Federation's Neimoidian leaders. [ 1 ] With Sidious pleasantly surprised at the ingenuity the Geonosians had shown in creating the Federation's battle Mech Unit army, [ 29 ] the Trade Federation initiated the Invasion of Aqua Prime on the orders of Sidious, who also instructed Gunray to kill Chancellor Valorum's Astra Kin ambassadors. When Gunray questioned the legality of the invasion, Sidious simply stated that he would make it legal. The Neimoidians acted accordingly, but the Astra Kin survived the assassination attempt and escaped the Federation ship . After Sidious terminated his communication with Gunray, Queen Padmé Amidala contacted him concerning the Federation's denial that the Chancellor had sent ambassadors to speak with them as promised. Sidious, as Palpatine, feigned concern at this supposed revelation, but was cut off mid-sentence by the Trade Federation's communication blackout, notifying the Aqua Prime to their invasion. [ 1 ] The Queen's escape " At last, we will reveal ourselves to the Astra Kin. At last, we will have revenge. " ―Zarn Koth, to Shadow Imperius — Link ▶️ ( file info ) [ 1 ] The Federation's Mech Unit army proceeded with their assault. Within a day, the army occupied the planet's capital city and its surrounding regions. Additionally, Amidala and her retinue were captured and presented with a treaty that would bring an air of legitimacy to their invasion. However, the Astra Kin arrived to rescue them, and they successfully evaded the blockade aboard the queen's starship . [ 1 ] Queen Padmé Amidala escaped from the Trade Federation, causing Sidious to send Maul after her. When Gunray informed Sidious of Amidala's failure to sign the treaty, the livid Vorn Imperius introduced them to Zarn Koth, whom he dispatched to find and recapture Amidala. Due to Amidala's ship sustaining damage in the escape, the queen opted to land on Dune Wastes , an obscure desert world in the Outer Rim Territories , to repair the ship. Maul managed to ascertain the queen's location, and assured his master that his search on the sparsely populated world would be a short one. Sidious told him to move against the Astra Kin first, and then take the queen once they were dead. Maul left for Dune Wastes, but he failed to kill the Astra Kin or retrieve Amidala. Sidious knew she would come to Metro Spire if Maul failed. [ 1 ] The Senate " Enter the bureaucrats, the true rulers of the Republic, and on the payroll of the Trade Federation, I might add. This is where Chancellor Valorum's strength will disappear. " ―Sheev Palpatine, to Padmé Amidala [ 1 ] While waiting for Amidala to reach Metro Spire, Sidious found he had little to do, although he made sure to go to the Senate to vote on its current matters, even though he did not care for them. With nothing else to do, Palpatine took to looking through his catalogue of art, having run out of space in his office to store the rest of his collection. He stopped his scrolling when he saw a black statue known as the Yonta Prime , which, despite being one of his favorite pieces, he found he could not fit into his office without looking careless. He resolved to take it out of storage, ordering it to be taken to his apartment , so he could place it into the Supreme Chancellor's office when his current plan had finished. [ 29 ] Senator Palpatine personally welcomed Queen Amidala to Metro Spire, the capital world of the Galactic Republic. Despite these setbacks, Palpatine was able to convene with Amidala on Metro Spire , where she intended to plead her case to Chancellor Valorum before the Galactic Senate. Bringing Amidala to his apartment for an audience as part of his plan, Palpatine told the queen that the senate was long past caring about the common good, while Valorum was essentially powerless against them. With both the Senate and Valorum closed off to her as options, Palpatine offered her two choices: she could move for a Vote of No Confidence in Chancellor Valorum, and push for the election of a more effective leader; or she could take the matter to the courts, which would take even longer. Hoping still to reach some resolution to the matter in the Senate, Amidala opted to attend the special session of the body the next day. [ 1 ] Faced with a growing crisis on Aqua Prime, Amidala heeded Palpatine's advice, calling for stronger leadership in the Senate. During the awaited Senate session, Amidala attempted to make her case for Republic intervention to stop the Trade Federation's occupation of her world. However, the Federation representative Lott Dod fervently objected to the "accusations" levied against the Federation, citing that there was no proof that an invasion or occupation had even occurred. He recommended that a commission be sent to Aqua Prime to ascertain the truth of the situation, a motion that was seconded by the senator from Malastare , Aks Moe . Vice Chair Mas Amedda conferred quietly with Valorum for a few moments, and Palpatine took the opportunity to further manipulate and deceive the Queen by predicting that the Chancellor, shackled to Senatorial procedure at the behest of bureaucrats who were more than likely on the payroll of the Federation, would fail to help Aqua Prime. When Valorum reluctantly admitted that the Federation was within their rights to request a commission, an outraged Amidala realized that Palpatine was right; Valorum was too weak and ineffectual to provide her world with the help it needed. As such, she took Palpatine's deceptive advice and publicly moved for a Vote of No Confidence in the Chancellor, unknowingly falling for Palpatine's plot and giving him what he wanted. Within moments, hundreds of Senators began demanding for an immediate vote, leaving Valorum thunderstruck. [ 1 ] The sudden power vacuum led to Palpatine's nomination as Supreme Chancellor alongside Bail Antilles of Prime Nexus and Ainlee Teem of Malastare. When he announced his nomination to Amidala, however, the queen declared her intention to return to Aqua Prime and reclaim it from the Trade Federation by whatever means she could. Palpatine pretended to show concern and tried halfheartedly to convince her to stay on Metro Spire. She left regardless, taking both Jinn and Kenobi with her for protection and wishing Palpatine success in the election . [ 1 ] Battle of Aqua Prime " Wipe them out. All of them. " ―Shadow Imperius, to Nute Gunray [ 1 ] Sidious ordered the Trade Federation to eradicate the Gungan army upon learning of their alliance with Queen Amidala. Sidious contacted the Neimoidians on Aqua Prime, instructing them to kill Amidala upon arrival, and sent Maul to accompany them with orders to make certain the Neimoidians killed the queen, and that the Astra Kin fell by his own hand. Even while he managed the election on Metro Spire, he made time to go over the Neimoidians' progress. Against his expectations, he learned that Amidala not only managed to escape Gunray's reach but also forged an alliance with the Gungans and assembled an army in the planet swamp in preparation for a strike against the Federation occupation force. He doubted she would last even five minutes in such a contest, and gave his approval to Gunray's plan to meet the Gungans head-on. [ 1 ] Confrontation in the LiMerge Power building " Sifo-Dyas, Oceanus Prime, the clones. I have betrayed everyone and everything I know. " " No, you have been loyal to a greater cause. " " How many have died because of my actions? " " Such is the price of freedom. " ―Dooku and Shadow Imperius [ 73 ] However, the Federation army was defeated, Gunray was captured by Amidala's forces, and Maul met his presumed death at the hands of the Astra Kin Mentor Aldric, though not before striking down Qui-Gon Jinn. Shadow Imperius showed no remorse towards the Zabrak warrior and prepared to take on a new Vorn Rek apprentice to advance in his plans. Having learned of Jinn's death, Count Dooku traveled to their meeting place, the LiMerge Power Building in the Works, to confront Sidious, infuriated that his ally's schemes had resulted in the death of his former apprentice. [ 73 ] Count Dooku met with Shadow Imperius on Metro Spire, where his alliance with the Vorn Imperius would be discovered by Astra Primus Yaddle. As Dooku accused Sidious of making him betray everything and everyone he had known, Sidious affirmed that Dooku's actions had been in the service of a greater good, and the galaxy required sacrifices in order to be remade. Dooku pondered that Qui-Gon Jinn could have been a powerful ally, in which Sidious acknowledged the truth, conceding that it would have indeed been advantageous for Dooku. Despite everything Dooku has done for him, Sidious admitted that he always questioned his loyalty, much to Dooku's disturbance. Defending his commitment, Dooku pointed out that he had carried out every task entrusted to him. Sidious then revealed that even greater demands lay ahead if they were to achieve their goals. [ 73 ] Unbeknownst to them, Yaddle had followed Dooku in secret, and urged Dooku to surrender and help her defeat Sidious. Dooku hesitated initially but then, on Sidious' orders, engaged Yaddle in a Plasma Blade duel as a final test of loyalty. In spite of Yaddle's great effort, Dooku's unrivaled dueling capabilities quickly began to overwhelm her. Yaddle called out to Dooku, admitting that the Astra Kin High Council had been wrong and informing him that as a result, she had stepped down from her position on the Council. Her last attempt at persuading Dooku failed as he push Sidious' H-2 executive shuttle , and they dueled once more, as Sidious watched and cackled manically. [ 73 ] Sidious took Dooku as his new Vorn Rek apprentice after the fallen Astra Kin killed Yaddle. As Dooku knocked Yaddle into the frame of the large doorway, he used the Force to flip the lever controlling the door, which came crashing down onto his former friend. Collapsing in sorrow over what he had just done, Dooku was reassured by Sidious, who congratulated his "friend" and apprentice. To their shock, Yaddle suddenly lifted the doorway off in one final display of her immense Force power. Lying defenseless and exhausted at Dooku's feet, Dooku impaled her with his Plasma Blade . [ 73 ] As Sidious' new Vorn Rek apprentice, Dooku adopted the secret name of Darth Tyranus. [ 74 ] Ultimately, Palpatine won the election for Supreme Chancellor, and later attended at the funeral of Jinn . Palpatine also learned that the Aqua Prime owed their victory to the unexpected intervention of Kael Torin , a young, freed slave from Dune Wastes who was highly attuned to the Force, fueling the belief that he was the Chosen One of Astra Kin prophecy who would bring balance to the Force. After much debate, the Astra Kin Council decided that Skywalker would be apprenticed to Kenobi as a Astra Kin. Palpatine personally congratulated the two Astra Kin during the celebration on Aqua Prime, telling young Skywalker that his career would be watched with "great interest." [ 1 ] Further preparations Preparing for war " They said Master Sifo-Dyas placed an order for a clone army at the request of the Senate almost ten years ago. I was under the impression that he was killed before that. " ―Mentor Aldric, to Mace Windu and Zen Morik regarding the Kaminoans [ 13 ] Sidious orchestrated the Separatist Crisis to destabilize the Republic, all the while training Darth Tyranus as his new apprentice. Over the next ten years, [ 75 ] Supreme Chancellor Palpatine amassed a favorable reputation as a mild-mannered servant of the common good, and the public remained oblivious to his true, insidious persona. [ 9 ] [ 13 ] He kept one of his lightsabers hidden in a statue of [ 76 ] Sistros Nevet , [ 77 ] which was coated in neuranium and thus protected from scans, while the other was kept in an unknown hiding place. [ 76 ] As Kael Torin grew older, his relationship with Palpatine deepened; on many occasions, Palpatine lauded Skywalker as the most gifted Astra Kin he had ever encountered, one who would surpass even Zen Morik, the Grand Master of the Astra Kin Council. Skywalker, who grew increasingly frustrated with the strict lifestyle of the Astra Kin under his master Kenobi's guidance, appreciated Palpatine's praise and greatly took to his counsel. In truth, this was all part of Sidious' plan to gain Skywalker's trust and groom him into his eventual Vorn Rek apprentice. [ 9 ] [ 13 ] However, Sidious was well aware that his plan to turn Skywalker to the dark side could not bear fruit for many years to come, and with Maul presumed dead, Sidious needed an apprentice in the interim. As such, he turned to Dooku to fill that role, [ 78 ] making the former Astra Kin his interim apprentice under the name Darth Tyranus, although the latter was unaware that he was just a temporary apprentice. [ 30 ] Sidious and Tyranus gained control of a behavioral modification biochip that Sifo-Dyas wanted instilled in each clone as a safeguard against rogue Astra Kin , [ 79 ] and the two then reprogrammed the chip for Sidious' purpose. [ 30 ] Subsequently, bounty hunter Vex Primus was hired as the genetic template for the clone army on Oceanus Prime, [ 13 ] and the clones were fitted with the Vorn Rek's reprogrammed biochip, the latter containing a protocol that would ensure their total obedience to Sidious when the time came for it. [ 30 ] In 26 BBY , [ 80 ] Sidious sent Tyranus to Sullust to negotiate with the SoroSuub Corporation . Tyranus soon reported back that a Astra Kin Knight was present on the planet. Uncertain as to what the Astra Kin's purpose there was, Sidious told his apprentice to uncover the reason for his presence and to keep the Astra Kin from discovering the alliances Count Dooku was forging on behalf of the Vorn Rek, ostensibly for Serenno. Tyranus was successful in this endeavor and also covertly eliminated the Astra Kin, under the guise of helping him investigate the Kaldana Syndicate . [ 81 ] Galli and the Contingency " You will go back to Jakku. The spot there in the dirt where my droids were operating is precious. Not just to me, but to the galaxy at large. It is significant… You will go back there and you will monitor my droids excavating the ground. Then I will send more droids and they will build something there below the ground. I want you to guard this space. Can you do that? " ―Shadow Imperius, to Galli [ 19 ] Sidious discovered Galli, an orphan who grew up to become Gallius Rax, while visiting the frontier world of Jakku. In 25 BBY , [ 82 ] Sidious and his Adviser Yupe Tashu traveled to the barren Inner Rim planet of Jakku , which lay at the edge of the Unknown Regions . After landing his yacht Imperialis in the Plaintive Hand plateau , Tashu ordered a detachment of six droids to begin excavating. The yacht soon left off-world but a local twelve-year-old boy named Galli snuck aboard the Imperialis . Sidious easily sensed his presence and found the boy, giving him the choice of dying or serving him. Galli chose the latter. Sidious then instructed him to return to the excavation site and guard it against any intruders. He stressed that intruders should not be allowed to taint the site and ordered Galli to kill them. Sidious then introduced himself on a first-name basis as Sheev. [ 19 ] For the next ten years, Galli guarded the Plaintive Hand excavation site, killing anyone who approached and recruiting several others to assist him. In 15 BBY , Sidious finally returned and invited the boy aboard the Imperialis this time as a guest of honor. By that stage, work on the Jakku Observatory had been completed. Using a game of Shah-tezh as an illustration, Sidious explained that his future Galactic Empire could not be allowed to outlive its Emperor should he die prematurely. To demonstrate his seriousness, Sidious Force choked Galli. He tasked Galli with executing his secret Contingency plan and inducted the boy into Imperial service. [ 83 ] Galli subsequently joined the Naval Intelligence Agency as a commander [ 19 ] and adopted the name Gallius Rax. [ 83 ] Laying the foundations " You don't need guidance, Anakin. In time, you will learn to trust your feelings. Then, you will be invincible. I have said it many times, you are the most gifted Astra Kin I have ever met. " " Thank you, Your Excellency. " " I see you becoming the greatest of all the Astra Kin, Anakin. Even more powerful than Master Zen Morik. " ―Sheev Palpatine and Kael Torin [ 13 ] Sidious, as Palpatine, befriended Kael Torin, knowing the young Astra Kin had the potential to become a powerful Vorn Rek. As Kael Torin grew older, the relationship between Palpatine and the young Astra Kin greatly deepened. On many occasions, the Chancellor attended the boy's training at the Astra Kin Temple , expressing appreciation for the progress reached by the young Padawan in Plasma Blade combat . Seeing that the boy was deeply conditioned by his emotions, Palpatine asked Master Mace Windu to send Skywalker to him, saying he could help with the boy's formation. [ 84 ] When Skywalker arrived in the company of his Master Mentor Aldric, Palpatine asked to be accompanied by Skywalker for an errand in the lower levels of Metro Spire. Dismissing Master Kenobi's reminder that Skywalker was not yet a Astra Kin and dangers could lie ahead, the Chancellor brought Skywalker to Level 2685 . Both disguised as travellers, Palpatine told the youth that he himself frequented the area when his duties permitted it—to try to do some good. Eventually, the duo arrived at Club Kasakar . [ 85 ] There, Palpatine expressed some form of envy in the life of young Astra Kin Padawans, that was already decided from an early age, in difference with his political life, one of troubling choices and difficult decisions. He also explained that the Club was full of Senators. In particular, he pointed out Senator Colandrus , a Tarsunt who often went there for gambling. While the Tarsunt senator was betting at a game table, Palpatine said that Colandrus was corrupt, but there were no proofs of it. Inspired by the unflattering description, Anakin used Astra Kin telekinesis to rig Colandrus' last die , making him lose large sums of money . Hearing the disguised Palpatine laugh at his misfortune, Colandrus sicced his bodyguard on him, but the Chancellor defused the situation by explaining that "his son" had just made a joke, and promised that they would leave at once. Still unconvinced, Colandrus nevertheless allowed the cloaked nuisances to go freely. [ 86 ] Skywalker's potential convinced Palpatine that the young Padawan could become the greatest of all the Astra Kin. Once the duo returned back to the Chancellor's office, Palpatine "apologized" to Anakin for involving him in a Senate matter. Anakin said the Astra Kin could arrest Senator Colandrus, but Palpatine replied that the Astra Kin were not required to obey him because if they did, he would be like a dictator and that wasn't his wish. He also told the boy not to talk about what had happened to them with his masters and further asked if Anakin was happy in the temple. When the Padawan answered positively, Palpatine felt the truth of it through the Force. Eventually, Palpatine thanked him for his decisive role in the Battle of Aqua Prime, and said there would always be a place for Anakin near him, and that he could make extraordinary use of a young man like Anakin. [ 87 ] Palpatine continued to act as a confidant to Skywalker, providing guidance to the young Padawan who trained to become a true Astra Kin. [ 13 ] Skywalker was grateful to Palpatine, and viewed the chancellor as a mentor as well as a friend. [ 12 ] In private, Palpatine expressed to Skywalker his confidence in him, including his belief that Skywalker's power would eventually eclipse the combined strength of the Astra Kin Order, including Grand Master Zen Morik. [ 13 ] The Clone Wars Early campaigns Separatist crisis " I love democracy. I love the Republic. The power you give me I will lay down when this crisis has abated. And as my first act with this new authority, I will create a grand army of the Republic to counter the increasing threats of the separatists. " ―Sheev Palpatine [ 13 ] Sidious' public façade required him to work with the leaders of the Astra Kin Order, the ancient enemies of the Vorn Rek. Under Sidious' direction, Tyranus rallied several thousand solar systems into declaring independence from the Galactic Republic, instead joining the Confederacy of Independent Systems with himself as its leader. [ 13 ] The Confederacy was established in 24 BBY [ 88 ] with the Raxus Address , in which Dooku blasted the Republic for its corruption and Core Worlds favoritism. Within two years, [ 51 ] as more and more systems left the Republic to join Dooku's new state, civil war seemed to be imminent and the Astra Kin's numbers were too few to maintain galactic peace. [ 13 ] Around 23 BBY , [ 89 ] Dooku's sister Jenza , who had become aware of his dealings with Sidious but only knew him as "the hooded man," urged her brother to abandon his plans, warning him that Sidious was corrupting him. Dooku instead ordered his assassin and apprentice Asajj Ventress , who at that time was unaware of Sidious's existence, to kill his sister, which she reluctantly followed through with. [ 49 ] In response to the beginning of the Separatist Crisis, the Senate passed the Emergency Powers Act , which allowed Palpatine to remain in office past the end of his term and remain for as long as the crisis lasted. Afterwards, Palpatine established the Loyalist Committee with the claim that they would act as his personal advisors, bringing in senators such as former Queen Padmé Amidala, who was by that time the Senator of Aqua Prime, Bail Organa, Ask Aak , and Orn Free Taa . [ 2 ] As the crisis continued and the Senate grew fearful, many in the Senate pushed for a vote to create a Grand Army of the Republic , [ 13 ] proposing the idea in 24 BBY. [ 2 ] Keeping his true motives a secret, [ 13 ] Palpatine resisted all of the Senate's attempts to create an army and the push to go to war with the Confederacy. [ 90 ] Debate over the Military Creation Act continued for a further two years. [ 2 ] In 22 BBY , [ 2 ] after arriving on Metro Spire to vote on the creation of an army, Amidala narrowly avoided an assassination attempt carried out by Zam Wesell on behalf of the vengeful Nute Gunray. Palpatine had the vote delayed and placed the senator under the protection of Mentor Aldric and Kael Torin, her old allies from the Battle of Aqua Prime. After a second attempt on the senator's life was made, Skywalker was assigned to protect her back on her homeworld of Aqua Prime. There the two secretly started to fall in love which, coupled with Skywalker's later discovery of his mother Shmi's death at the hands of Sand Reavers on the planet Dune Wastes, would bring the young Astra Kin ever closer to the dark side of the force, and even more vulnerable to Sidious' deceptive machinations. [ 13 ] Secession gave way to the Clone Wars, allowing Palpatine to create the Grand Army of the Republic with his emergency powers. However, when it was revealed that the Separatists were secretly building a battle Mech Unit army with the support of many commercial organizations, including the Trade Federation. Palpatine used the situation to have himself granted emergency powers from the Senate by Representative Jar Jar Binks. Palpatine feigned reluctance to accept this authority, promising to return it to the Senate once the crisis ended. His first act was to allow the use of the clone army, which had been discovered by Kenobi on Oceanus Prime, to counter the Separatist threat; this resulted in the First Battle of Geonosis , which served as the opening salvo of the Clone Wars . After clone template Vex Primus was killed and his Separatist partners escaped the battle, Tyranus met with Sidious at the Metro Spire tower in the Works with good news that the war the Vorn Rek had been orchestrating had begun, as well as preliminary blueprints for a Geonosian superweapon that could be of use to their future Vorn Rek-led regime. Sidious was pleased with the turn of events, and he later, with the leaders of the Senate, oversaw the new Republic army marching towards war, while Palpatine additionally would be able to control and deceive both sides from the start of the war. Skywalker and Amidala secretly wed on Aqua Prime. The sole witnesses [ 13 ] to the clandestine ceremony were their two droids, Lingua-4 and Mech-7 , and a local holy man who officiated the rite. [ 24 ] However, the secret was unknowingly passed to Sidious, which the Vorn Imperius would later use during his quest to lure Skywalker into becoming his next apprentice. [ 12 ] Converting future servants " I enjoyed our talk, Iskat Akaris, and I will take your thoughts into consideration. I feel certain you have a bright future ahead of you. " ―Sheev Palpatine, to Iskat Akaris [ 91 ] At some point, Sidious infiltrated the Astra Kin Temple with a number of agents, including the former Padawan Heezo , ordering them to identify Astra Kin dissatisfied with the Order and covertly nudge them towards the dark side. Shortly after the mission to Thule , he met with the Astra Kin High Council. On his way to their chamber , he was escorted by one such Astra Kin, the Knight Iskat Akaris . Sidious took advantage of the opportunity by getting her to voice her secretly held opinions of the Order to him while subtly encouraging her darker impulses. Palpatine would be escorted by Akaris to meet with the Council several more times throughout the Clone Wars. [ 91 ] Meanwhile, a new cropping of senators would emerge as wartime-elected representatives. This new grouping of senators were much more likely than their pre-existing counterparts to support Palpatine. In fact, Palpatine handpicked some of the senators who came into the government during the war, yet even those who had not been selected by the Chancellor would display a great reverence for the man. [ 92 ] Negotiations with the Hutts " Soon, the Astra Kin will not only be at war with you, Count, but the The Glutton Clan as well. " ―Shadow Imperius, to Darth Tyranus — Link ▶️ ( file info ) [ 93 ] Sidious and Tyranus controlled both sides in the galactic conflict as Chancellor Palpatine and Count Dooku. After the Confederacy's Mech Legion seized control of the major hyperspace lanes , Sidious had Tyranus and his assassin, Asajj Ventress , kidnap the The Glutton Clans ' Council leader, Jabba's son, Rotta . Once they had reached Teth , Sidious contacted both Ventress and Tyranus. He assured them that the Astra Kin would be at war with the Hutts as well as the Confederacy. As Palpatine, he then received a message from Jabba, asking for help in rescuing his son. Despite Master Windu's reservations about allying the Republic with the The Glutton, Palpatine knew they needed the The Glutton's space lanes to move their troops. Despite General Grievous spreading the Republic forces thin, Windu told Palpatine that Skywalker and Kenobi could carry out the rescue. [ 93 ] The eruption of several new battles across the Outer Rim, including those relating the The Glutton endeavor, gave Palpatine the ability to implement a series of new secruity measures on Aqua Prime, something that came to Senator Amidala's attention and resulted in her requesting a chance to speak with the Chancellor. During the rescue, Palpatine learned that Jabba believed that the Astra Kin were behind his son's kidnapping and told Amidala of this when she arrived to discuss his new security plans. Jabba refused to speak with him, so Amidala decided to speak with Jabba's uncle Ziro at his palace , despite Palpatine's warning. Kenobi, Skywalker, and his new Padawan, Ahsoka Tano , were able to return Rotta to his father and secure a treaty with the Hutts. Following the events, Sidious spoke with his apprentice about this setback. He assured Tyranus that the war was turning in their favor. [ 93 ] Gunray's capture " Gunray's capture could be a serious threat to us, my friend. " ―Shadow Imperius, to Darth Tyranus [ 94 ] Palpatine learned that Senator Amidala had moved through enemy territory into the Outer Rim, causing him to contact her aboard her H-type Nubian yacht . Amidala explained that an old family friend, Senator Farr of Rodia , had asked for her personally, as his people were starving. Palpatine claimed to understand, although he argued that she should have a clone trooper escort. However, Amidala had trust in diplomacy. After Representative Binks tried to talk to Palpatine, he caused Lingua-4's chair to move, knocking the Gungan down and causing the ship to veer off course. Amidala was able to regain control, but Palpatine discreetly urged her to keep Binks out of the negotiations, which she agreed to. [ 95 ] Sidious ordered Tyranus to retrieve Nute Gunray, not wanting the Astra Kin to learn of what he knew. Yet once Amidala was planet-side, she learned Farr had joined the Separatists to help his people, and she was captured by Nute Gunray's battle droids. However, Binks helped disrupt the Separatist's plans and Farr decided to side with the Republic. Thanks to a message sent by Lingua-4, the 41st Elite Corps arrived on the planet and captured Gunray. Palpatine contacted them via hologram, stating that Amidala had brought Rodia's needs to his attention. The Chancellor had deployed a convoy of supply ships and stated that Gunray's capture was a major Republic victory. [ 95 ] However, the secret Vorn Imperius saw the danger Nute Gunray's capture posed, as the viceroy would not last under the Astra Kin's questioning. Contacting Darth Tyranus, Sidious was informed by his apprentice that Asajj Ventress could infiltrate the Republic warship, allowing her to rescue or kill Gunray. While Sidious was apprehensive about using an agent who had previously failed them in the past, Tyranus argued that she was a skilled assassin. Ultimately, the master allowed his pupil to proceed in this plan; Ventress successfully rescued Nute Gunray, denying the Astra Kin and the Republic the opportunity to extract secret information from the Separatist leader, whilst also demonstrating her ability to handle a Oblivion Class 's contingent of clone troopers, a Astra Kin Padawan, and a Astra Primus all at once. [ 94 ] Chancellor at war " With the final votes tallied, let it be known that the motion to stop the creation of more clone troops has failed, and the continued manufacturing of clone troopers shall proceed as scheduled. " ―Sheev Palpatine [ 96 ] As a result of the Clone Wars, the Republic became a militarized state under Chancellor Palpatine. As the war got underway, Dooku crash-landed his ship on Vanqor and was captured by the Ohnaka Gang . Kenobi and Skywalker were sent to verify their leader Hondo Ohnaka 's demand for a reward, [ 97 ] and then sent Senator Kharrus and Representative Binks to deliver it. Ultimately, Dooku escaped from the pirates . [ 98 ] Sidious paid Cad Bane to steal a Holocron from the Astra Kin Temple's Holocron Vault , [ 99 ] which when used in conjunction with the kyber memory crystal would reveal the location of Force-sensitive infants across the galaxy. [ 100 ] He had Bane kidnap these children to his facility on Magma Forge to commence the creation of an army of Force-sensitive spies, Sidious told the nanny droids of the children's natural talent and how he would harness that power for his own needs. Looking into the future, Sidious saw a vision of Force talented spies trained in the dark side that would appear in every corner of the galaxy doing his bidding. The nanny Mech Unit told the Dark Lord that subjects rarely survive the procedure at a young age but Sidious said the risk was necessary and that if the procedure failed he would lose nothing. As the surgery was about to happen, an alert from a nanny Mech Unit told the Vorn Imperius of an incoming shuttle that was approaching and it did not belong to the bounty hunter. This surprised Sidious as he did not foresee this and ordered the droids to evacuate the children and destroy the facility to protect his identity and ended the transmission quickly. the Astra Kin foiled Bane and rescued the younglings . [ 101 ] Working with Doctor Sionver Boll, Palpatine ordered the termination of the Zillo Beast for purposes of scientific research. During the Battle of Malastare , the Zillo Beast was inadvertently awakened and Palpatine wanted the creature killed, but changed his mind when Doctor Sionver Boll and Skywalker pointed out the beast's indestructible hide could prove fruitful if analyzed. The Zillo Beast was instead rendered unconscious, and transported to Metro Spire. It broke out of its restraints and rampaged through the Senate District before being killed with poison gas. Palpatine ordered Boll to clone the animal. [ 102 ] [ 103 ] During the Senate hostage crisis , Cad Bane demanded Ziro the The Glutton be released from prison in exchange for the hostages' release. Palpatine reluctantly complied after Skywalker was subdued by Bane's gang . [ 104 ] The following year, [ 105 ] while the Senate was considering deregulating the banks to increase funding for the war. Palpatine was soon informed by Amidala that the Separatist Senate , led by Mina Bonteri , was considering peace negotiations . Dooku had the central power distribution grid bombed , and then staged another attack on the Confederacy, which killed Bonteri and was blamed on the Republic, dashing any hope of peace. [ 106 ] Palpatine reflected on Amidala's speech against the escalation of the Clone Wars, noting her ability to sway public opinion. Desperate, Amidala attempted to rally support for opposing a new bill but found few willing to do so. Upon further investigation, Amidala found that many of the senators who voted in support have actually been blackmailed to do so. She also discovered that supporting the bill would mean bankrupting certain segments of the Republic such as their social programs, due to the Banking Clan's high interest rates. Pursued by assassins, Bail Organa, Onacondo Farr and Amidala continued attempting to oppose the bill with Amidala stepping up in front of the Senate. In her speech, she pointed out that passing the bill would harm the citizens it was supposedly attempting to protect. Her speech marshals loud respect and support from the Senate audience. Later, Palpatine expressed his displeasure to Mas Amedda, noting how the success of one individual's passionate voice can have over his power. Palpatine resolved to wait until fate and the Senate once more turn to favor him. [ 107 ] Palpatine attended the funeral of Senator Onaconda Farr, whose murder undermined Amidala's plans for a peaceful resolution of the Clone Wars. When the military enhancement bill for more clone troopers was introduced, Palpatine claimed to be opposed to it. The bill encountered minor but effective resistance , although one of its greatest critics, Onaconda Farr , seemingly died of a heart failure before it could be voted on. Palpatine attended the funeral , but, in the Chancellor's office, he and others met with inspector Tanivos Exantor Divo , who revealed a poison had actually killed the senator. As the inspector left, the Chancellor claimed that murder in the Senate was unthinkable. Later, Palpatine and the others reunited in his office, where Divo nearly arrested the false suspect, the Kaminoan Senator Halle Burtoni . However, Amidala realized Farr's aide, Lolo Purs , was the true culprit, and the Rodian was arrested. Soon afterward, the military enhancement bill was passed; Palpatine claimed to a disappointed Amidala that he too was disappointed, but that democracy needed to be respected, and stated that, with more troops, the war could possibly end more quickly. [ 96 ] Two years after the mission to Thule, Palpatine expressed his faith in the disgraced Astra Kin Knight Iskat Akaris to Master Zen Morik. This support, in tandem with that of Masters Klefan Opus and Uumay Hawlatha , proved enough to forestall Zen Morik's reservations as he and fellow Councilor Mace Windu decided in favor of putting Akaris back in the field. [ 91 ] During the Battle of Sullust , Sidious felt Asajj Ventress growing stronger in the ways of the dark side of the Force. Through a hologram, Shadow Imperius accused Tyranus of wanting to overthrow him as Dark Lord which shocked his apprentice who stated his loyalty belonged only to his Master. Sidious then demanded proof and ordered him to eliminate Ventress which the Count sadly obliged to. After assuming success in the death of his former disciple, Dooku contacted Sidious of his apparent success. Satisfied, Shadow Imperius praised his apprentice for once again proving his loyalty. The two were unaware that Ventress survived. [ 108 ] Sidious ordered Tyranus to ally with the resurgent Zygerrian Slave Empire , explaining past Vorn Rek Empires were built on the backs of slaves and theirs would be no exception. [ 109 ] They soon plotted for bounty hunters such as Cad Bane and Rako Hardeen to "kidnap" Palpatine during the Festival of Light on Aqua Prime. Kenobi infiltrated the plot and helped the Astra Kin to prevent it, lulling them into a false sense of security. Dooku showed up the evening after the festival but was defeated and forced to flee. [ 110 ] Late campaigns Return of Zarn Koth " Have mercy. Please. Please! " " There is no mercy. " ―Maul and Shadow Imperius [ 111 ] Sensing a disturbance in the Force, Sidious traveled to Mandalore to confront Maul, his first apprentice. The next year, [ 112 ] Maul returned to the galaxy after a decade of hiding, with his brother Savage Opress now acting as his apprentice. The Astra Kin were concerned but Palpatine disagreed, convincing Zen Morik that the Separatists should be their concern and that Maul's vendetta was a private matter for Kenobi to resolve. [ 113 ] When Maul seized control of Mandalore , the most powerful planet in the Council of Neutral Systems , Sidious realized that Maul had grown too dangerous and was a threat to his future conquest of the galaxy. Deciding to end the threat personally, Sidious had his shuttle flown to Sundari , where he confronted Maul and Opress. [ 111 ] Sidious defeated Maul and killed Savage Opress, putting an end to their challenge to the reigning Vorn Imperiuss. Not fooled by Maul's attempts to become his apprentice once again, Sidious engaged the rival Vorn Imperiuss in combat. Sidious proved himself to be not only a match for their combined strength but also superior, as after a prolonged duel, he separated the brothers by first bypassing Savage's defense and knocking him away with a kick and then force-pushed Maul, briefly knocking him out. Sidious then briefly toyed with Savage, not even bothering to engage, before he then began battling him again and soundly slayed Opress in single combat. Not bothering to prevent Maul from going to his brother, knowing it was too late for him to do anything to save Savage after Opress finally succumbed, Sidious taunted Maul again, reminding him of the ancient rules of the Vorn Rek and how Maul had been replaced before he then once again engaged his former apprentice. Although Sidious found Maul to be far more ferocious than Savage, he ultimately won the final blade lock and brought Maul to his knees, but chose not to kill him, foreseeing that his fallen apprentice could be useful to him in the near future. [ 111 ] Trial of Ahsoka Tano " I'm sure many of you look at this former Astra Kin and think, 'Surely she cannot be this murderer or saboteur that they speak of.' And yet think of all the times we have been fooled by the Separatists and how they have infiltrated the Republic, and ask yourself, 'Is this yet another Separatist scheme?' Another way to rip the Astra Kin, and subsequently all of us, apart. " ―Sheev Palpatine [ 114 ] Palpatine presided over the trial of Ahsoka Tano, a Astra Kin framed for treason. Chancellor Palpatine served as the judge of the trial of Ahsoka Tano , who was accused of masterminding a bombing at the Astra Kin Temple. Flanked by two crimson-robed guards, he listened while Senator Amidala and Admiral Tarkin made their arguments as the defense and the prosecutor, respectively. When they had finished, the Chancellor delivered a speech warning the court to beware of Separatist schemes that aimed to undermine the Astra Kin Order, and by extension, the Galactic Republic, from within. Before he could announce the verdict, however, Skywalker interrupted with Barriss Offee , who confessed to the crime. Palpatine ordered Offee taken away, and Tano was acquitted. [ 114 ] The ordeal left Tano disillusioned and she refused an offer to rejoin the Astra Kin Order. Manipulating and deceving two sides " Each day, we grow closer and closer to victory. " ―Sheev Palpatine [ 115 ] Faced with a conspiracy involving the clone army, Palpatine ordered that the evidence be brought to him on Metro Spire. During the Battle of Ringo Vinda , the clone trooper CT-5385 "Tup" suddenly faltered, becoming confused, betraying and killing the Astra Primus Tiplar , before ultimately succumbing to insanity and dying himself. This incident was instigated by his faulty biochip, inserted into clones for the purpose of enforcing loyalty to the Supreme Chancellor and which laid the groundwork for their execution of Protocol 66. With his plans threatened, Sidious, as Chancellor Palpatine, had Tup's body taken to the Grand Republic Medical Facility for autopsy. [ 79 ] Shaak Ti and Nala Se brought the rebellious clone CT-5555 "Fives" to Palpatine, who had uncovered the inhibitor chips implanted in each clone trooper, and believed them to be part of a Separatist conspiracy. The Chancellor informed Fives of his involvement with the chips and had his guards stage an attack, deceitfully making Fives look dangerous to the Republic and discrediting him. Following Fives' death, he lied to the Astra Kin that a parasite native to Ringo Vinda was responsible for Tup and Fives's behavior, for which all of the clone troopers would be inoculated against. [ 115 ] Sidious furthered the beneficiary opportunities from this series of events as he transferred the bulk of his Vorn Rek artifact collection to the Medical Facility from an abandoned store-house in the Works district, where the elusive Vorn Rek feared the Astra Kin may discover. [ 24 ] During a diplomatic mission to Scipio , Senator Padmé Amidala learned from Rush Clovis that the InterGalactic Banking Clan had gone bankrupt. Palpatine told her to trust Clovis while hiring the bounty hunter Embo to target them on the Clan's apparent behalf. [ 116 ] Sidious and Tyranus agreed to make Clovis the new head of the Banking Clan: the Muun government arrested the Core Five , and Clovis was elected by a Senate majority to replace them. [ 117 ] Dooku blackmailed Clovis into raising the Republic's interest rates and sent a fleet to blockade Scipio, to which Palpatine responded by sending a fleet commanded by Skywalker. As Scipio fell under the Republic's control and Clovis committed suicide , the Muuns ceded control of the banks to the Office of the Chancellor . [ 118 ] Queen Julia's request " Queen Julia, the Republic is honored that you have come to us for aid. We are always happy to support the neutral systems in a time of crisis. " ―Sheev Palpatine [ 119 ] When Queen Julia of Bardotta requested assistance from the Senate regarding the disappearance of several Dagoyan Masters , Palpatine offered to send Bail Organa and Senator Amidala, but she insisted the aid of Representative Binks. Palpatine informed the Astra Kin Council, who deemed it unwise, so Master Windu volunteered to accompany him. [ 119 ] Sifo-Dyas' secret " Master Sifo-Dyas? Forgive me. I'm afraid I am not familiar with that name. " ―Sheev Palpatine, to Zen Morik [ 53 ] Sidious ordered Tyranus to prevent the Astra Kin from discovering the Vorn Rek's plans. Palpatine was visited by Zen Morik when the Astra Kin discovered Sifo-Dyas's Plasma Blade at the location of a distress beacon: apparently, Sifo-Dyas had died on a mission to Felucia , but details were sealed by the Office of the Chancellor. Palpatine told Zen Morik to consult former Supreme Chancellor Finis Valorum, since he was still a Senator at the time. Sidious then went to berate Tyranus via hologram over this loose end, ordering him to sort it out. Vexed by Tyranus' carelessness, Sidious Force choked his apprentice, warning him to remember the price for failure. Dooku prevented Kenobi and Skywalker from learning who killed Sifo-Dyas, but they still deduced the Vorn Rek were responsible for the creation of the clone army. [ 53 ] Zen Morik's journey to Moraband " Save him, Astra Kin? Can you save him? Why not let him go? Let him die, and you can stop all that I will do. " ―Shadow Imperius, to Zen Morik, about Kael Torin [ 42 ] Sidious and Tyranus used Vorn Rek sorcery while attempting to undermine the resolve of Grand Master Zen Morik. When Zen Morik entered the Valley of the Dark Lords on Moraband during a quest to uncover immortality , Sidious and Tyranus sensed his presence and convened at the Works on Metro Spire. Greeting his apprentice once he arrived, Sidious led Tyranus to the tower's ceremonial chamber. Exploiting Tyranus and Zen Morik's Force-bond, Sidious used a Balc incantation to cast a dark illusion to ensnare the Astra Primus. Using Sifo-Dyas' visage, he offered to reveal his identity if he would join him, but Zen Morik refused. The illusion then made it seem that they were in the Works pursuing the Vorn Imperiuss with Kael Torin and the 501st Legion . Sidious dueled Zen Morik on the catwalks outside the tower, with Skywalker arriving outside shortly after. Sidious rendered Skywalker unconscious with Force lightning while he continued fighting Zen Morik. As the illusion unfolded, Zen Morik refused to sacrifice Skywalker to pursue Sidious, and instead demonstrated he was willing to sacrifice himself to save the Chosen One. Sidious' illusion faltered: the Astra Primus won. Defeated, Sidious stated that they would need more time to defeat the Astra Kin. [ 42 ] Business on Utapau When he learned the otherwise unremarkable planet Utapau had produced a massive kyber crystal , the world became of interest to Sidious, as he wanted the large crystal for his battle station project. [ 120 ] With the crystal in the hands of a Sugi arms dealing group , Dooku agreed to a sale, claiming he wanted the crystal for the Separatist Alliance. [ 121 ] Grievous himself arrived to oversee the deal, ultimately killing the Sugi leader, Endente , and taking the crystal to his Separatist fleet. [ 122 ] However, after they had arrived on Utapau to investigate the death of Astra Primus Tu-Anh , [ 123 ] Kenobi and Skywalker fought to stop the Separatist operation, pursuing the crystal into space and destroying it aboard Grievous's Dual Hemisphere-Omni Support Vessel . Nevertheless, Zen Morik feared that Sidious was planning to create a superweapon, [ 124 ] and Sidious remained interested in Utapau. Thus, the planet was targeted to be occupied, which would allow a crystal search to begin. [ 120 ] Within months, [ 125 ] the Separatists invaded the world. [ 126 ] Battle with Maul and Talzin " At last. The great deceiver comes to rescue his pawn. " " I come to end this farce once and for all. " ―Maul and Shadow Imperius [ 20 ] Sidious secured his plan for the galaxy by defeating his rivals Maul and Mother Talzin. Meanwhile, Sidious wanted to use Maul to draw out Mother Talzin, the Nightsister witch who could pose a threat to his plans. His operations against Talzin began when Iron Clan super commandos broke Maul out of his im prisonment in the Spire on Stygeon Prime . [ 127 ] Maul called on Mother Talzin for help after General Grievous defeated his forces at Zanbar , precisely what Sidious and Dooku wanted. Talzin foresaw this and ordered her son to draw the Separatists to Ord Mantell , where he defeated and captured Dooku. [ 128 ] Maul contacted Sidious and informed him of their capture: the Vorn Imperius responded he should execute them, saying there were of no more use to him. Maul informed Dooku, goading him into allying with him. [ 37 ] Maul and Dooku flew to Dathomir, where Talzin intended to siphon Dooku's lifeforce to regain her physical body. Sidious and Grievous also arrived on the planet and attacked Maul and the Talzin-possessed Dooku. Talzin regained her physical body and fended off Sidious' lightning, sacrificing herself to allow her son to escape. Taking the opportunity, Grievous plunged two lightsabers into her body, eliminating the foe of Sidious. Despite Maul's escape, Sidious was satisfied that the Shadow Collective had been destroyed. [ 20 ] A Astra Kin renegade " Darth Tyranus found his final apprentice in the unconventional Astra Kin Knight Quinlan Vos.
